- **audit-agent-skills**: Read-only health audit for installed Agent Skills across Claude and Codex. Use when the user asks to inspect, validate, review, troubleshoot, compare, or inventory AI skills; check SKILL.md structure, trigger descriptions, safety signals, portability, references, duplicate names, likely trigger conflicts, or Claude/Codex compatibility; or wants a deterministic report before sharing or installing a skill. Never changes skills and never executes bundled scripts. ([workspace-labs/agent-health-checks](https://github.com/workspace-labs/agent-health-checks/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-agent-skills) · ★1)
- **audit-agent-skills**: Checks whether agent skills have correct source ownership, installed-content parity, and Claude symlinks. Use only when explicitly asked to audit or verify agent-skill installations. ([prasanthkumaar/agent-skills](https://github.com/prasanthkumaar/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-agent-skills))
- **audit-agent-skills**: Audit, organize, archive, and restore local Agent Skills with skill-manager. Use when users ask to inspect installed skills, find duplicates or stale skills, understand usage evidence and scan coverage, clean up skill directories, archive candidates safely, or restore archived skills. ([Ryan-yang125/skill-manager](https://github.com/Ryan-yang125/skill-manager/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-agent-skills))
- **audit-agent-speed**: Measure and fix a slow coding-agent setup: status line, hooks, instruction bloat, effort, worktree pile-up, antivirus and indexer scans. Use when "Claude is slow" or "CPU is pinned". App speed → audit-performance. ([kensaurus/skills](https://github.com/kensaurus/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-agent-speed) · ★10)
- **audit-agent-stuck-behind-login-wall-af-8f38bc1b4e5a7411**: Fixes audit agents stalling behind login walls with wall detection and re-authentication recovery. Use it when session expiry redirects the agent to login mid-crawl. Not for initial login failures, which are credential problems. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-agent-stuck-behind-login-wall-af-8f38bc1b4e5a7411))
- **audit-agent-stuck-in-a-loop-requesting-7d1f8d7c4fccfe74**: Breaks agent missing-receipt request loops with attempt limits and escalation. Use when an agent repeatedly requests the same missing receipt. Not for receipts that arrive late. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-agent-stuck-in-a-loop-requesting-7d1f8d7c4fccfe74))
